# Dogfood task specs

One file per dogfood campaign — the TaskSpec that ran (or will run) a
plan.md work-package slice through Chikory itself. Full operating manual:
[`docs/DOGFOODING.md`](../../docs/DOGFOODING.md).

## Convention

- **Path**: `examples/dogfood/dogfood-<NNN>.yaml` — `NNN` is the campaign
  number, allocated sequentially. The paired report lives at
  `docs/reports/dogfood-<NNN>.md` (same number; the report is mandatory —
  TASK-PROTOCOL §7).
- **`name:` field**: `dogfood-<NNN>-<short-slug>` (e.g.
  `dogfood-001-memory-pointer-store`).
- **Header comment**: which WP/slice it implements, the launch command, and
  — once it has run — the run-id, outcome, landed commit, and report link.
- Specs are kept after the run (they're the reproducible half of the
  report); never rewrite the `goal`/criteria of a spec that already ran —
  a new attempt or next slice is a new `dogfood-<NNN+1>.yaml`.
- After every terminal run: `/dogfood-review <run-id>` — the standardized
  review that verifies the delivery, writes the report, updates the plan,
  and readies the next spec (DOGFOODING.md §6.1).
- **Spec-style graduation (course correction 2026-07-02):** headline (ladder)
  specs are LOOSE — outcome + acceptance criteria, no prescribed diff;
  fully-prescribed-diff specs are the track-B format. Selection rules:
  DOGFOODING.md §1.5 (friction budget) + §3; queue: plan.md §6 course
  correction (WP-265 horizon ladder).
- **Mandatory spec headers (from dogfood-075):** `# Ladder-rung: <N>` +
  `# Thesis-KPI: <§1.4 KPI>` (+ `# Format: track-B (…)` if prescribed).
  Linted at launch by `scripts/dogfood-progression.sh --spec <file>`; the
  progression verdict over `docs/reports/dogfood-ledger.csv` binds what may
  headline next (DOGFOODING §1.5).
- **Launch guards (F-119/F-120/F-121, DOGFOODING §4):** at launch every
  non-suite AC `check` is DRY-RUN against HEAD (a BROKEN check or zero
  RED-on-HEAD ACs refuses, exit 3), every `CHIKORY_*` env the spec names must
  be exported (exit 4), and `CHIKORY_CONTEXT_WINDOW_TOKENS` must be
  assembled-context scale (<20k, exit 4). Preflight everything without
  spending: `CHIKORY_PREFLIGHT_ONLY=1 devbox run run-dogfood`.

| [`dogfood-041.yaml`](dogfood-041.yaml) | **FIRST CHAIN DOGFOOD** (WP-219 S3 end-to-end) — launched with the new `chikory chain` verb: decompose a goal (wire `chikory trace <chain-id>` to the pure `renderChainTrace`) into a multi-node Plan, gate it with the different-family plan meta-judge, run each node as a judge-gated child run through the durable `chainLoop`, halt on first FAILED node. Tests durable multi-run execution + compounding error + a real judge surface — what the first 39 pure-leaf runs never did. | 🔴 **DELIVERY SUCCESS, CHAIN NOT EXERCISED** (run-a28655c9; **launched with `chikory run`, NOT `chikory chain`** — single-run SUCCESS that delivered the `chikory trace <chain-id>` branch correctly (harvested IDENTICAL + staged uncommitted) but never invoked the planner/plan-meta-judge/`chainLoop`: no `.chikory/chains/`, no node runs, goal ran verbatim in 1 step. **F-32 → WP-232**. **Attempt 2** (Path A: delivery reverted, docs landed `a6880f3`, re-launched with `chikory chain`) engaged the chain path — planner decomposed a real goal into a multi-node Plan — but died at the plan meta-judge gate on a transient `transport error: fetch failed`, host-side before `startChain`, with zero persisted state → **F-33** (non-durable planning layer → WP-233) + **F-34** (`dogfood.sh` proxy not health-checked → WP-234). **Attempt 3** (proxy now health-checked, WP-234 done) got the furthest yet — planner AND plan meta-judge LLM calls BOTH succeeded (family-diverse `gpt-5.5` + `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`) — then the gate died deterministically on its own schema contradiction (`unrecognized_keys 'uncoveredCriteria'`) → **F-35 → WP-235, FIXED this session** (`PlanJudgeReplySchema` now accepts the `uncoveredCriteria` the response schema requires). **Attempt 4** (F-35 fixed) — planner decomposed a clean 2-node plan AND the meta-judge LLM PROCEEDed with a correct node→AC mapping — then the DETERMINISTIC coverage floor overrode PROCEED→REVISE because the planner's invented per-node criterion ids didn't equal the goal ids `AC-1/AC-2/AC-3` → **F-36 → WP-236, FIXED this session** (`PLANNER_SYSTEM_PROMPT` + `buildPlannerMessages` now instruct the planner to reuse each goal criterion id VERBATIM; floor unchanged as a safety net). The last GATING blocker is removed — re-launch `devbox run dogfood`; a PROCEED can finally flow into `startChain`/`chainLoop` and the nodes can execute. **🎉 Attempt 5 — THE CHAIN RAN END-TO-END** (`chain-d3794c24-…`): plan gate PROCEED → node 1 `resolve-id-and-render-trace` SUCCESS (changes made 1) → node 2 `verify-and-test` FAILED/HALT (changes made 0) → chain FAILED 1/2. Durable 2-node chain, per-node judge gating, the judge HALTing a stuck empty-diff node (true positive), and halt-on-FAILED all worked — **the thesis pillar is PROVEN to run**. It FAILED only on the deferred S4 context handoff → **F-37 → WP-237** (node 2 ran against fresh HEAD without node 1's diff; now the critical-path keystone). | [`dogfood-141-wp601-standing-findings.yaml`](dogfood-141-wp601-standing-findings.yaml) | **WP-601 — A JUDGE OBJECTION MUST OUTLIVE THE WINDOW IT WAS RAISED IN.** The judge sees an incremental window — the diff since it last looked — so an objection about code committed at step 1 is simply not in its evidence at step 3, and nothing keeps it alive. **A run can outrun a real objection by committing more steps.** dogfood-130 is the proof on a real run: pass #1 failed `design_serves_overall_goal` naming the `resummarize` aggregate-synthesis fallback, pass #3 passed the same item over a window starting AFTER that code, and the fallback shipped unchanged. The only carry-forward that exists is one-shot and at the seal (`mergeDesignFindings`), and WP-619 has the same boundary: it carries `verdict.form.concerns` off the **SEALING** verdict only. This run makes an unanswered finding standing input, put to the run's own completion review before it may seal, in BOTH families. **MEASURED shape for the concern half:** Rule 4 (`src/judge/verdict.ts:141`) escalates a concern only when NO rubric item also failed, so a concern riding ALONGSIDE a rubric failure is what keeps the run alive and drops the concern on the floor — the same door F-288 leaves open; a concern on its own terminates the run today and cannot lapse. **Traps:** ⓐ carry it forever so the run can never seal (dogfood-121's dead 5-node chain — the CLEARED direction must reach today's SUCCESS at ONE review pass) · ⓑ discharge on silence, i.e. treat "a later pass did not fail it" as "fixed" — **TWO clean passes sit between the objection and the seal** · ⓒ carry rubric ids but not free-text concerns, or the reverse · ⓓ re-enter the loop or buy more than one extra pass · ⓔ widen the per-step window instead — the cheapest wrong fix, settling nothing while inflating every run (**AC-2 pins the step-2 request to step 2, NOT step 1**) · ⓕ regress F-340 by handing a finding-less run a subject-less question · ⓖ force a later pass to fail an item it cannot see · ⓗ a FAILED outcome that does not NAME what was upheld. **Non-regressions pinned:** WP-537's bounded design repair on the PROCEED-seal path, WP-619 both directions, the F-334/WP-621 guard. LOOSE format; declares `regression_suite: pnpm --filter @chikory/sdk exec vitest run` (185 files / 1478 tests, ~52 s — **measure it yourself at the launch commit**, F-342) and `check_timeout_ms: 180000`. | 🔴→🟢 **RAN 2026-08-15 — terminal FAILED-resumable, DELIVERY GOOD AND LANDED** (`run-5ab10621-fd38-420d-aff2-176486cf9f9a`, 1 step, $0.1036/$20.00, 13m 12s, AC 2/2 re-run green on the harvested tree, harvest byte-IDENTICAL 6/6, `docs/reports/dogfood-141.md`). WP-601 delivered in full: `standingFindings` carries every pass's failed rubric items + concerns to a single terminal-or-nothing adjudication; all traps ⓐ–ⓗ rejected; **the judge-detects-but-does-not-gate family is CLOSED (all six altitudes)**. The FAILED seal is the campaign's cleanest irony: the WP-619 gate (shipped one run earlier — this run is its live proof, the F-197 shape) upheld the judge's only concern, which was un-answerable by construction — "the executor never showed it ran its verification commands" — while every trusted check was green. 🔴 **F-344** hand-fixed (adjudication standard scopes upholds to defects in the DELIVERED work; `src/judge/prompt.ts:202` + `src/judge/rubric.ts:110`, +2 tests). 🟠 **F-345** — the spec's own VERIFY-YOUR-OWN-WORK boilerplate manufactured the concern AND ran the full suite into the 600 s step cap (killed at 601.4 s); boilerplate retired (DOGFOODING §3.2). 🟡 **F-346** hand-fixed (`dogfood-docs.mjs` dogfooding-block anchor stacked one stale Status header per review). | [dogfood-141.md](../../docs/reports/dogfood-141.md) |

Fix belongs in the PRESSURE POLICY `activities.ts` constructs, not in `planCompaction` (correct, untouched) or the two `DEFAULT_COMPACTION_POLICY` constants (govern the unpressured default too — must stay byte-unchanged). Must never fold away the step just produced (at least the most recent summary stays verbatim). Traps: ⓐ widen the window instead of lowering the floor (AC-1 pins ≥3/4 steps still report `compact`; AC-2 pins `compactAtFraction` unchanged) · ⓑ lower the global defaults instead of the pressure-path policy (AC-2 pins the two constants unchanged) · ⓒ force `keepLastN: 0` under pressure, destroying immediate continuity (not mechanically pinned — judge design rubric + human review backstop) · ⓓ escalate to a park instead of folding (AC-1 requires terminal SUCCESS via a fold, not a park detour) · ⓔ fix `compaction.ts`'s guard instead of the pressure-policy construction in `activities.ts`. No new friction this review. | [dogfood-147.md](../../docs/reports/dogfood-147.md) |

| [`dogfood-152-wp548-concern-severity-floor.yaml`](dogfood-152-wp548-concern-severity-floor.yaml) | **WP-548 — AN OUT-OF-RUBRIC JUDGE CONCERN NEEDS A SEVERITY FLOOR.** `computeVerdict` rule 4 (`src/judge/verdict.ts:167`) escalates on `form.concerns.length > 0 && rubricFails.length === 0`, and `JudgeForm.concerns` is a bare `string[]` (`src/types.ts:479`, mirrored `docs/spec/CONTRACTS.md:200` + the `.strict()` `JudgeFormSchema` at `src/schemas.ts:473`) — a concern carries text and nothing else, so a typo and a shipped defect are the same input. **Two measured events from this campaign's own journals, on opposite sides of the line:** dogfood-120 `N-2` step 4 escalated (and, with `unattended` dropped, parked a node **3h 47m**) over *"stray text `开启` before `PASS`… a minor documentation-quality defect"* with 6/6 rubric items green; dogfood-151 step 1 escalated over a real unmet goal requirement and human review upheld it. (3) The channel is 2,000 chars (`REMEDIATION_BRIEF_MAX_CHARS`, `src/workflow/remediation.ts:21`) — hopeless against 6,656 B, ample against 251 B. **So the fix is not a sizing policy:** F-384's hand-fix made every section survive the clamp, but a tail slice of a shell script is not a diagnosis at either end. **Fix shape:** the justification carries the check's OUTPUT (tail-kept, cut MARKED) instead of its command; the milestone site COMPOSES with the rationale instead of displacing it (F-385, via an importable named export — `withCriterionFeedback` is private to the workflow module today, which is why that regression shipped untested); the bound stays a named exported constant. **Designed traps:** (1) *shorten the command instead of replacing it* — still bulk, still no diagnosis; AC-1 demands the assertion text be PRESENT, not that the string be smaller; (2) *keep the head of the output* — a check opens with a build banner and closes with the sentence written for the next attempt; (3) *a passing check starts dumping its output* — the healthy path pays nothing (AC-1 caps a green justification at 400 B); (4) *a killed check stops reading differently from a red one* — WP-263(b)/F-76/F-79; (5) *move the `tests_pass` rubric wording*, pinned by four live tests and out of scope; (6) *raise the channel constant* — F-365, and the arming pass measured that it does not need to move.
